# The interactive seat stopped mid-work, and this is what it was holding

**Filed automatically by `background/seat_continuity.py`, not by a person.** The seat ran no
tool for **0.4h** and its process is gone. It did not stop on purpose: an
interactive session that finishes says so, and this one just stopped — which is the shape an
Anthropic API error leaves behind, four times now by the director's count.

This document exists so that nobody has to notice. It is a staged doc, so the next worker tick
draws it like any other work.

## DISPOSITION 2026-08-29 — ADOPTED in part; this alarm was the publish wedge

**Not archived. Read this before re-drawing it.**

The 12:24 episode above was not a latent tidiness problem: it was the cause of a ~2h PUBLISH-GATE
WEDGE (3 recorded failures, then a 4th at `6c0271d17` while the diagnosis was in flight).

**The chain.** The C1/C3 schematic corrections reached the front door's ALT TEXT in
`site/index.html` and were committed. The two surfaces carrying the same correction —
`docs/design/canon_claims.yaml` (the register) and `site/assets/model-on-a-page.svg` (what a
sighted reader actually meets) — plus the register's literal membership pin in
`tests/tools/test_canon_drift_check.py`, were the work this seat was holding when it died. They
stayed uncommitted from 12:24. At HEAD the register's anchor sentences were therefore no longer on
their page, so
`test_every_live_claim_is_bound_to_a_sentence_still_on_its_page` reported UNBOUND and blocked all
publishing.

**Why nobody saw it in the tree.** The publish gate runs pytest inside a `git archive HEAD`
checkout. The working tree was GREEN the whole time and measured nothing — the unstaged half was
exactly what made it pass. Running the single test in the shared tree reproduces nothing; the
refutation is `git archive HEAD | tar -x` and run there.

**Adopted, per this document's own Adopt branch** — the edit was sound, the transport failed:
committed as `ec376c60d` (canon_claims.yaml + the SVG + the membership pin, landed together via
`tools.surgical_land`). 32/32 green at HEAD in the gate's own checkout shape.

`surgical_land` refused the first attempt, and its refusal was the measurement: landing the register
without the pin is the silent scope-shrink `EXPECTED_CLAIM_IDS` exists to catch. The renamed claims
keep their predicate and only flip `expects` present→absent, so they stay keyed to the channel
rather than to today's verdict — the checker returns SUPERSEDED the day `green_stance` or
`collateral_call` is wired into `simulation/`.

**What is still owed, and why this stays live.** Only the canon-drift subset was adopted. The other
paths this alarm lists — `company/risk/counterparty_collateral_desk.py`, `saas/capital/solvency.py`,
`saas/reporting/annual_report.py`, `sim/scenario/gas_scenario_generator.py`,
`tools/run_value_cycle_ab.py` and the rest — belong to other lanes and remain uncommitted. Archiving
this document now would claim work that did not land. It is archivable when those are committed or
reverted, not before.

**The general lesson, for the next reader.** A seat that dies mid-work does not merely leave a
messy tree: if what it was holding is *half* of a two-surface repair, it wedges whatever gate reads
the committed half. Correcting one rendered surface and not the other is half a fix, and the half
left behind is the one that blocks publishing.

## DISPOSITION 2026-09-02 — drawn as a publish-gate wedge suspect, REFUTED as the cause, re-frozen

Drawn by the worker tick that cleared the 80-minute publish-gate wedge, where
`.publish_gate_state.json` listed this document in `cited_findings` alongside two others.

**It was not the cause, and this is not an opinion.** The red set at the wedge was enumerated as
exactly ONE test (`.last_gate_blocking_tests.json`, `total_red: 1`, census `complete` at
`f7714a356`): `tests/controls/test_daemon_loop_mutation.py::test_dms_isolated_accounts_for_every_check_run_cycle_calls`.
That test was reproduced red at HEAD `452132c8e`, its cause traced to `580c47101` adding
`_check_worktree_reap()` to `run_cycle()` without moving the fixture's neutralise-list, fixed, and
landed as `cd0dc6eb1` (gate-rc 0, 20/20). Nothing in this document is on that path. Being CITED as a
suspect and being the CAUSE are different things, and the citation was a coincidence of timing —
these three findings were simply the ones open when the wedge began.

**Re-frozen rather than archived, because its own archive condition is genuinely unmet.** This
document says: *"Archive to `docs/staging/done/` once the paths above are either committed or
reverted."* They are not. `git status` still shows uncommitted source paths, and the document has
accreted 23 instances and five "still live" days since 2026-08-26 — the condition it names is
ongoing, not stale. Archiving it tonight would be filing a live alarm as done, which is the failure
this project keeps paying for.

**What is owed, stated so the next drawer inherits a decision rather than a re-read:** the adopt/
discard call on the 2026-08-26 snapshot's uncommitted paths. That is a real piece of work and it is
not the wedge's, so it was not done under a priority-zero tick.
